Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The seismology variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 94 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 574 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_94(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 94. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^574(S, F) is trivial. QED.
